| GET | /uploads/:name | async (req,res,next) => {   if (db.backend !== 'mysql') return next();   try {     if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$/.test(req.params.name)) return res.sendStatus(404);     const file=await db.getFile(req.params.name);     if (!file) return res.sendStatus(404);     res.set({'Content-Type':file.content_type,'Cache-Control':'private, max-age=3600','X-Content-Type-Options':'nosniff'}).send(file.payload);   } catch (error) { next(error); } } | server/src/index.js:96 |
| GET | /api/health | (req, res) => {   res.json({     status: 'healthy',     service: 'EDGEWFORCE Enterprise Backend',     version: '1.0.0',     timestamp: new Date().toISOString()   }); } | server/src/index.js:128 |
| GET | /api/health/ready | async (req, res) => {   try {     if (!db.isCloud && isServerless) throw new Error('A cloud database is required.');     if (db.isCloud) {       const result = await db.rpc('application_release_readiness', {});       if (result?.release !== '2026-10-15' \|\| !result?.integer_ids \|\| !result?.collaboration \|\| !result?.activity_monitoring) throw new Error('Database upgrade is required.');       if (process.env.WORKFORCE_GEOFENCE_ENFORCED === 'true' && !result.workplace_access) throw new Error('Workplace access migration is required.');       if (process.env.WORKFORCE_REPORTS_ENABLED === 'true' && !result.executive_reports) throw new Error('Executive report migration is required.');       if (process.env.NEXUS_ENABLED === 'true' && !result.nexus) throw new Error('NEXUS migration is required.');       if (process.env.HR_DAILY_REPORTS_ENABLED === 'true' && !await db.rpc('hr_reporting_readiness', {})) throw new Error('Daily HR reporting migration is required.');     } else { await db.ready(); await db.find('users', {}, { limit: 1 }); }     res.json({ ready: true, release: '2026-10-15' });   } catch (err) {     logger.error('Database readiness check failed: ' + err.message);     res.status(503).json({ ready: false, message: 'Database configuration or upgrade requires attention.' });   } } | server/src/index.js:136 |
| GET | /api/cron/hr-daily-report | async (req,res,next)=>{   res.set('Cache-Control','no-store');   const expected=process.env.CRON_SECRET,provided=req.get('authorization')\|\|'';   if(!expected\|\|expected.length<32\|\|Buffer.byteLength(provided)!==Buffer.byteLength(`Bearer ${expected}`)\|\|!timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(provided),Buffer.from(`Bearer ${expected}`))) return res.status(401).json({error:{message:'Unauthorized scheduler.'}});   try{const results=await hrDailyReportService.daily();res.status(results.some(r=>r.error\|\|r.results?.some(d=>['failed','uncertain'].includes(d.state)))?503:200).json({results});}catch(e){next(e);} } | server/src/index.js:180 |

## Every target database table and column

The JSON companion preserves full column definitions and table metadata. PostgreSQL migrations remain authoritative for PostgreSQL functions, policies, checks and grants.

### ai_conversations

- `id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true}`
- `user_id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true,"references":{"table":"users","column":"id"}}`
- `title`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","default":"Sales Copilot Session","required":true}`
- `context_data`: `{"format":"jsonb","required":false}`
- `created_at`: `{"format":"timestamp with time zone","type":"string","default":"now()","required":true}`

### ai_messages

- `id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true}`
- `conversation_id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true,"references":{"table":"ai_conversations","column":"id"}}`
- `role`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","required":true}`
- `content`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","required":true}`
- `created_at`: `{"format":"timestamp with time zone","type":"string","default":"now()","required":true}`

### announcements

- `id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true}`
- `company_id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true,"references":{"table":"companies","column":"id"}}`
- `title`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","required":true}`
- `message`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","required":true}`
- `category`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","default":"General","required":false}`
- `priority`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","default":"NORMAL","required":false}`
- `target_audience`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","default":"ALL","required":false}`
- `author_name`: `{"format":"text","type":"string","required":false}`
- `pinned`: `{"format":"boolean","type":"boolean","default":false,"required":false}`
- `created_at`: `{"format":"timestamp with time zone","type":"string","default":"now()","required":true}`

### attendance

- `id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true}`
- `company_id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true,"references":{"table":"companies","column":"id"}}`
- `employee_id`: `{"format":"integer","type":"integer","required":true,"references":{"table":"employees","column":"id"}}`
- `date`: `{"format":"date","type":"string","default":"CURRENT_DATE","required":true}`
- `clock_in`: `{"format":"timestamp with time zone","type":"string","required":false}`
- `clock_in_time`: `{"format":"timestamp with time zone","type":"string","required":false}`
- `clock_out`: `{"format":"timestamp with time zone","type":"string","required":false}`
